Reproduce: Meat Boy

Every agent below was trained with one of the seeds 1234, 2025, 31337, the seeds behind the published numbers. Each one is a folder with its network (best.npz) and the settings it trained with (state.json). Levels are named as the game's files and the commands name them (0 is the first level of an indexed game).

1. Set up

git clone https://github.com/Code-SorceryLab/PEAK-DRL-Tool.git
cd PEAK-DRL-Tool
git checkout 07efc9b02354b6af3781dab6a661e275eb7d5e41
pip install -r requirements.txt

2. Get the agents

All 1242 agents of this game: agents/meatboy.zip. Unpack it into the repository: unzip meatboy.zip -d agents. Or take one agent's two files from the table.

3. Replay an agent

The replay command in the table opens the live dashboard at http://127.0.0.1:8000/ and plays the agent with its own persona, reaction time and seed. An agent trained on a mutant replays on that mutant: its command sets PEAK_MUTANT first (on Windows, run set PEAK_MUTANT=... on its own line before it).

4. Train it again

The retrain command trains the same persona, level, seed and generation budget from scratch. The AST evaluation's agents retrain all three seeds on their mutant at once.

5. Rebuild the results

python -m code.neuro.ast_eval play --game meatboy           # playtests: one attempt per agent
python -m code.neuro.ast_eval play --game meatboy --replay  # the original's frozen agents on each mutant
python -m code.stats.ast_metrics --page                     # the mutation metrics, and this site
python -m code.stats.ast_paper                              # the paper's tables and figures
python -m code.neuro.command_center --open                  # this Command Center, on your machine

Every agent

SourcePersonaLevelSeed FilesReplayRetrain
